### [1] [Event-Driven Language Models with Sparse Neural Activity for Neuromorphic Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30439)
*Simon Richter,Ruhai Lin,Jason Yik,Taylor Kergan,Rui-Jie Zhu,Farshad Moradi,Jason Eshraghian*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在重度量化线性注意力模型中诱导稀疏神经活动的方法，通过在神经形态多核平台上利用事件驱动执行将非结构化稀疏性转化为吞吐量优势，相比边缘GPU上的Transformer推理可实现最高37倍吞吐量提升和16倍功耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer大语言模型推理受限于内存绑定的KV缓存和二次方注意力代价；状态空间模型虽通过线性注意力和固定大小循环状态缓解了这一问题，但其大型稠密线性投影即使在量化后仍计算开销巨大。GPU架构无法利用非结构化稀疏性，需要寻找能同时解决计算效率和稀疏性利用的新平台和方法。

Method: 提出一种在重度量化线性注意力模型中诱导稀疏神经活动的方法：通过每个投影可训练阈值（±Δ）将低于阈值的激活置零，同时保留关键异常值，在最小性能损失下实现稀疏化。该方法面向多核多芯片神经形态平台，利用事件驱动执行将非结构化稀疏性在计算和通信两个层面转化为吞吐量收益。

Result: 在保持与稠密模型可比性能的同时，有效算术操作减少至1/4；在神经形态平台上相比边缘GPU上的可比Transformer推理，吞吐量最高提升37倍、功耗降低16倍；相比非稀疏化基线提升最高5.4倍。

Conclusion: 稀疏且量化的线性注意力模型与事件驱动多核平台天然契合，为在大模型推理中兼顾效率、功耗和性能提供了一条有前景的部署路径，弥补了GPU架构在利用非结构化稀疏性方面的根本性不足。

Abstract: Inference with transformer-based large language models (LLMs) is often limited by the memory-bound KV cache and quadratic attention cost. State-space models (SSMs) mitigate this through linear attention and fixed-size recurrent states, but their large dense linear projections remain computationally expensive even after quantization. We introduce a method that induces sparse neural activity in heavily quantized linear-attention models with minimal performance loss. Activations below a per-projection trainable threshold ($\pm Δ$) are nullified while preserving crucial outliers, achieving comparable performance to dense models with up to 4$\times$ fewer effective arithmetic operations. Targeting a multi-core, multi-chip neuromorphic platform, where event-driven execution converts unstructured sparsity into throughput at both the compute and communication levels, a capability GPU architectures fundamentally lack, we project up to 37$\times$ higher throughput and 16$\times$ lower power versus edge GPU inference of a comparable transformer-based model, and up to 5.4$\times$ improvements over the non-sparsified baseline. These results position sparse, quantized linear-attention models as a natural fit for deploying LLMs on event-driven multi-core platforms.

</details>


### [2] [Conjoint Audio-to-Spikes Encoding and Processing for Efficient Neuromorphic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30792)
*Valentin M. Meunier,Amélie Gruel,Pierre Lewden,Adrien F. Vincent,Sylvain Saïghi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向FPGA硬件实现的可编程非学习型神经形态音频编码器，并与脉冲神经网络分类器协同优化，在TIMIT数据集上实现了首个端到端神经形态脉冲编码与评估，在Heidelberg Digits基准上达到99.77%的分类准确率，超越了该基准上的神经形态最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态传感器与脉冲神经网络结合可降低AI能耗，但当前原生神经形态数据集稀缺，需要软件工具将输入数据转换为脉冲。然而高度仿生的模拟器在数字硬件上难以实现，因此需要一种可硬件实现的高层可编程编码方案。

Method: 采用非学习型、高层级可编程的神经形态编码器，目标在FPGA上实现，将音频信号编码为脉冲序列；同时联合优化编码器与分类器，并使用基于定量脉冲活动的硬件无关指标评估管线效率。

Result: 在前馈网络中，对脉冲编码的Heidelberg Digits数据集达到99.77%的分类准确率，超过该基准数据集上的神经形态最先进水平；同时实现了更低的学习与推理能耗。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了TIMIT数据集的端到端神经形态脉冲编码与评估，证明了可硬件实现的非学习型编码器与SNN分类器联合优化能够在降低能耗的同时提升分类性能。

Abstract: Obtaining data from neuromorphic sensors and processing it with Spiking Neural Networks is a promising solution to lower the energy cost of artificial intelligence. The current rarity of natively neuromorphic datasets promotes the development of software tools to translate input sensory data into spikes. However, highly bio-mimetic simulators can be challenging to implement on digital hardware. In this work, we evaluate the neuromorphic encoding and subsequent classification of audio into spikes using a non-learnable, high-level, programmable encoder targeting hardware implementation on FPGA. We quantify the pipeline's efficiency with hardware-agnostic metrics based on the quantitative spiking activity. Our study focuses on the simultaneous optimisation of encoder and classifier: the first provides efficient and informative data so that the latter achieves a better performance with an overall lower energy cost at learning and inference. This work introduces the first end-to-end neuromorphic spike-encoding and evaluation of the TIMIT dataset. Our simple feedforward network reaches a classification accuracy of 99.77% on a spike-encoded Heidelberg Digits, overcoming the neuromorphic state of the art on this benchmark dataset.

</details>
